**[00:04:56.260] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000032)  
Magandang umaga po sa ating lahat.

**[00:04:58.180] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000033)  
As we begin our deliberations on the proposed budget or budgets of the Movie and Television Review and Classification Board,

**[00:05:08.280] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000034)  
the Optical Media Board, the Film Development Council of the Philippines, and the Philippine Racing Commission,

**[00:05:14.520] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000035)  
I'd like to emphasize that these agencies may appear to operate in very different fields,

**[00:05:21.580] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000036)  
but they share one important responsibility, protecting the public,

**[00:05:28.180] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000037)  
interests while supporting industries that contribute to our economy, culture, and livelihood of our people.

**[00:05:35.280] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000038)  
Ang budget po ng pamahalaan ay pera po ng taong bayan.

**[00:05:39.080] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000039)  
We all know that.

**[00:05:40.520] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000040)  
Kaya sa bawat pisong ilalaan natin sa mga ahensya ninyo,

**[00:05:44.020] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000041)  
dapat malinaw kung saan ito gagamitin, ano ang kongkretong resulta, at paano makikinabang ang ating mga kababayan.

**[00:05:52.260] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000042)  
Let's tackle, first, the budget of the MTRCB.

**[00:05:56.840] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000043)  
For MTRCB, we have...

**[00:05:57.240] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000051)  
For MTRCB, the agency was given under 2026 General Appropriations Act, a total budget of 75.29 million pesos.

**[00:06:06.780] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000052)  
For fiscal year 2027, it is requesting a total budget of 49.99 million.

**[00:06:15.260] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000053)  
Today, Filipinos are consuming content not only through traditional television and cinema,

**[00:06:22.620] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000054)  
but also through streaming platforms and various digital channels.

**[00:06:27.240] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000055)  
Our children, in particular, have unprecedented access to content.

**[00:06:33.540] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000056)  
We, therefore, need to ask whether a regulatory framework is keeping pace with these changes.

**[00:06:57.240] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000057)  
Second, the Optical Media Board.

**[00:07:03.660] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000058)  
For OMB, the agency was given under the 2026 General Appropriations Act, a total budget of 47.03 million.

**[00:07:11.020] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000059)  
For fiscal year 2027, it is requesting a total budget of 53.462 million.

**[00:07:19.460] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000060)  
The media landscape has dramatically changed.

**[00:07:24.040] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000061)  
CDs and DVDs...

**[00:07:27.240] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000062)  
We have moved into a digital environment where content can be copied, downloaded, distributed, streamed, and reproduced almost instantly.

**[00:07:36.880] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000063)  
This raises an important question.

**[00:07:38.740] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000064)  
Is the Optical Media Board still operating with the mandate and systems appropriate for today's digital economy?

**[00:07:47.160] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000065)  
We need to make sure that public funds are being used efficiently,

**[00:07:51.020] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000066)  
that the agency is responding to actual threats to intellectual property and legitimate businesses.

**[00:07:57.660] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000067)  
Third, the Film Development Council of the Philippines.

**[00:08:02.000] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000068)  
For FDCP, the agency was given under the 2026 General Appropriations Act, a total budget of 367.35 million pesos.

**[00:08:12.660] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000069)  
For fiscal year 2027, it is requesting a total budget of 311.74.

**[00:08:20.140] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000070)  
The Philippine film industry is not merely an entertainment industry.

**[00:08:23.880] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000071)  
It is part of our cultural identity.

**[00:08:27.240] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000072)  
It is also a potential source of jobs, tourism, investment, and international recognition for Filipino talent.

**[00:08:35.420] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000073)  
We have Filipino filmmakers, actors, writers, directors, animators, cinematographers, editors, production workers, and other creative professionals

**[00:08:44.740] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000074)  
who have demonstrated that we can compete internationally.

**[00:08:49.720] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000075)  
But government support must translate into real opportunities for Filipino workers and filmmakers.

**[00:08:57.240] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000076)  
We should therefore examine how FDCP's program are benefiting independent filmmakers,

**[00:09:04.000] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000077)  
regional filmmakers, young artists, and emerging talents that only establish names in the industry.

**[00:09:12.760] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000078)  
Finally, the Philippine Racing Commission or FILRACOM.

**[00:09:16.700] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000079)  
For FILRACOM, the agency was given under the 2026 General Appropriations Act,

**[00:09:21.580] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000080)  
a total budget of 199.76 million pesos.

**[00:09:26.620] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000082)  
For fiscal year 2027, it is requesting a total budget of 202.85 million pesos.

**[00:09:34.540] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000083)  
The horse racing industry is not simply about betting or entertainment.

**[00:09:39.160] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000084)  
It involves breeders, trainers, jockeys, veterinarians, farm workers, racetrack employees,

**[00:09:47.620] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000085)  
and thousands of families whose livelihoods are connected to the industry.

**[00:09:51.500] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000086)  
Ibig sabihin po, nakapagbibigay po siya ng trabaho.

**[00:09:55.600] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000087)  
We therefore need to ensure that FILRACOM is effectively regulating the industry,

**[00:10:00.680] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000088)  
protecting the integrity of horse racing,

**[00:10:04.260] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000089)  
and making sure that the government revenues generated from the sector are properly accounted for and maximized.

**[00:10:11.980] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000090)  
I would therefore like to know whether FILRACOM proposed budget is sufficient to strengthen regulation,

**[00:10:19.120] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000091)  
improving monitoring, modernize its system,

**[00:10:21.720] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000092)  
and ensure transparency in the collection and utilization of racing revenues.

**[00:10:28.480] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000096)  
These four agencies operate in different sectors, but they face a common challenge.

**[00:10:33.480] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000097)  
The world is changing faster than our regulatory and support systems.

**[00:10:38.480] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000098)  
Horse racing is becoming more technologically driven.

**[00:10:43.480] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000099)  
Media piracy is becoming increasingly digital.

**[00:10:47.480] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000100)  
Television and film consumption are moving toward online platforms.

**[00:10:52.480] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000101)  
And the creative economy is becoming increasingly global.

**[00:10:58.480] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000107)  
Therefore, our budget deliberation should not simply ask how much money does the agency need.

**[00:11:04.480] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000108)  
We should ask more important questions like,

**[00:11:08.480] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000109)  
what will the Filipino people get in return for the budget given to you?

**[00:11:15.480] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000110)  
Every allocation must have a corresponding public benefit.

**[00:11:19.480] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000111)  
We need stronger accountability, measurable outcomes, efficient use of resources,

**[00:11:24.480] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000112)  
and programs that directly benefit Filipino workers, consumers, artists, and entrepreneurs.

**[00:11:32.480] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000113)  
At the end of the day, ang budget po ay hindi lamang numero sa papel,

**[00:11:36.480] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000114)  
buhay, trabaho at serbisyo publiko ang nakasalalay dito.

**[00:11:41.480] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000115)  
Kaya ngayong deliberasyon, I look forward to hearing from your agencies,

**[00:11:47.480] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000116)  
your respective offices, their concrete plans, their accomplishments, their challenges,

**[00:11:53.480] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000117)  
and most importantly, how they intend to ensure that every peso entrusted to them

**[00:11:58.480] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000118)  
by the Filipino people will be put to good use.

**[00:12:02.480] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000119)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[00:12:16.480] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000120)  
Okay, on that note, let us proceed with the briefing proper.

**[00:12:21.480] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000121)  
For the orderly procedure,

**[00:12:22.480] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000122)  
we will have the following chronology of budget presentation.

**[00:12:26.480] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000123)

**[00:16:01.100] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000170)  
For the budget utilization rate, from 2020 to 2025, the MTRCB steadily improved its budget utilization with obligation rates rising from 70.23% in 2020

**[00:16:16.820] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000171)  
to a peak of 97.72% in 2023,

**[00:16:57.700] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000176)

**[00:17:09.780] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000177)

**[00:17:18.720] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000178)  
The agency's record of efficient fund release once obligations are made.

**[00:17:23.840] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000179)

With a target to earn 96%.

**[00:19:22.460] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000190)  
I'm happy to share, Mr. Chairman, we attained 130.46%, or an equivalent of 125.224 million total revenue as of December 31, 2025.

**[00:19:39.320] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000191)  
In fiscal year 2025, review fees accounted for the majority of our revenue at 77.26%.

**[00:19:48.720] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000192)

**[00:19:58.060] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000193)  
Revenue collection in January to June 2025 reached 67.97 million, achieving 70.80% of the 96 million target.

In January to June 2026, despite a higher target of 98 million,

**[00:20:18.720] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000195)  
collections declined to 62 points, a 5.36 million decrease in actual collections,

**[00:20:34.260] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000196)  
and a 6.92 percentage point drop in efficiency, signaling weaker revenue performance year on year.

**[00:20:45.920] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000197)  
In fiscal year 2025, the MTRCB,

**[00:20:48.720] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000198)  
achieved strong physical performance across key indicators.

**[00:20:52.860] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000199)  
Compliance remained high, with 99.90% of entities under our jurisdiction adhering to MTRCB rules.

**[00:21:02.300] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000200)  
Material classification targets were fully met,

**[00:21:05.060] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000201)  
with 171,972 television programs, trailers, and related materials reviewed and classified by the board,

**[00:21:16.600] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000202)

**[00:21:18.720] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000203)  
In the end of the 10-day period.

**[00:21:21.120] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000204)  
On case resolution, 83.33% were resolved within 90 days, slightly below the 96% target.

**[00:21:31.140] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000205)

**[00:21:37.520] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000206)

**[00:21:42.040] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000207)

**[00:21:48.720] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000209)  
dissemination activities against a target of 50, further advancing our responsabling panonood advocacy.

**[00:24:03.640] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000224)  
Breakdown across all seven RCD at categories, January to June 2025 versus 2026, TV plugs fell sharply.

**[00:24:14.120] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000225)  
Movie trailers for television grew more than 130%.

**[00:24:19.740] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000226)  
The television program's submissions, PG-rated content dominates both years.

**[00:24:28.620] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000227)  
The total volume declined slightly from 65,769 to 59,981, but the SPG-rated content grew.

**[00:24:41.240] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000228)  
For the registered entities by title...

**[00:24:44.120] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000229)  
By 2025 versus 2026, cable television registrations fell by 161.

**[00:24:51.360] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000230)  
The largest shift, totally registered entities, declined from 2,495 to 2,341.

**[00:25:03.060] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000231)  
Cable television registrations fell from 1,056 to 895, the largest driver of the overall decline in registered entities.

**[00:25:14.120] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000233)  
This reflects the industry's ongoing shift from cable delivery to internet-based services,

**[00:25:22.060] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000234)  
with several operators voluntarily surrendering their NTC Certificate of Registration and Provisional Authority as they exit cable operations.

**[00:25:34.880] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000235)  
For the developmental activities for fiscal years 2025 and 2026,

**[00:25:41.960] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000236)  
the MTRCB is not only...

**[00:25:44.120] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000237)  
a regulatory body, but is also a developmental agency.

**[00:25:48.980] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000238)  
Beyond classification, it plays a proactive role in advancing media literacy, responsible viewership, and industry growth.

**[00:25:57.900] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000239)  
Through partnerships, capacity-building programs, and public information campaigns,

**[00:26:03.160] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000240)  
the board helps shape a media environment that protects audiences while fostering creativity and innovation.

**[00:26:14.120] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000241)  
The board's Responsabling Panonood campaign.

**[00:26:16.980] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000242)  
The board's responsible viewing advocacy is especially important in the recent incidents of school violence this year,

**[00:26:26.740] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000243)  
that unfortunately led to the loss of young lives.

**[00:26:31.000] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000244)  
Through the Responsabling Panonood program, the MTRCB aims to help parents guide the media consumption of their children,

**[00:26:38.780] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000245)  
while encouraging creatives to produce media content,

**[00:26:42.900] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000246)  
that entertains and educates the youth on contemporary Filipino values.

**[00:26:48.800] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000247)  
The Responsabling Panonood program is carried out through diverse initiatives,

**[00:26:53.980] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000248)  
including seminars, film classics for children,

**[00:26:58.020] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000249)  
evergreen film showings, Responsabling Panonood pulong and infomercials.

**[00:27:03.960] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000250)  
It also features creative approaches, such as Responsabling Pagbabay and Pagguhit,

**[00:27:09.660] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000251)  
ensuring that media literacy and responsible viewership are supported in the MTRCB campaign.

**[00:27:12.720] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000254)  
viewership are promoted across

**[00:27:14.700] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000255)

**[00:29:50.260] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000332)  
Okay. Well, I have

**[00:29:52.260] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000333)  
several questions here for you,

**[00:29:54.260] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000334)  
Chairwoman.

**[00:29:56.260] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000335)  
First,

**[00:29:58.260] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000336)  
on online regulation.

**[00:30:03.000] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000337)  
If Congress expands MTRCB's mandate to online platforms, kasi po ngayon parang marami na pong lumalabas pag binuksan mo ang TV, may mga Netflix, et cetera, et cetera, social media and online games, is MTRCB ready technologically, financially and organizationally to assume that responsibility?

**[00:30:27.060] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000338)  
First, Mr. Chairman, I would like to emphasize that the MTRCB is not for prohibition but for regulation.

**[00:30:38.300] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000339)  
We will accept the challenge if the legislators would include the said online services, streaming platforms,

**[00:30:51.480] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000340)  
the subscription video on-demand services in our jurisdiction or our provision.

**[00:30:57.060] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000341)  
However, it would entail a lot of coordination with the said stakeholders, such as the OTTs, the over-the-top, and the online streaming services.

**[00:31:12.500] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000342)  
Kasi pag minsan, late at night, when I turn my TV, when I get home, I watch some movies.

**[00:31:20.340] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000343)  
Nakakagulat na lang, bigla na lang may kubot-kubot na babae dyan.

**[00:31:26.040] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000344)  
And I didn't know that.

**[00:31:27.060] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000345)  
A young man, my son, behind me was like looking also.

**[00:31:32.660] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000346)  
So, I don't know.

**[00:31:35.020] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000347)  
Kung nangyari nga sa akin, maybe sa ibang mga household na wala naman kasing restriction when you turn on dahil subscription po yun.

**[00:31:44.160] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000348)  
So, yun po yung tinatanong natin, can we do something about it?

**[00:31:47.640] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000349)  
I mean, kaya nga, if we're given by Congress an expanded mandate, can you do it?

**[00:31:54.340] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000350)  
And when can you do that?

**[00:31:57.060] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000351)  
Mr. Chairman, if I may, it would be, yes, it would be feasible.

**[00:32:04.700] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000352)  
Yes, the MTRCB can do it.

**[00:32:06.880] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000353)  
But I would like to reiterate that it is only the regulation that we can do.

**[00:32:11.040] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000354)  
I'd like to clarify that the movies that will be exhibited in the online streaming platforms do not need to go through prior constraint,

**[00:32:21.280] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000355)  
which is what we are really pushing for, even in the past hearings.

**[00:32:26.400] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000356)  
I have observed.

**[00:32:27.060] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000357)  
Already mentioned, with regard to the online streaming and the subscription video on-demand services,

**[00:32:33.040] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000358)  
what we need from them is to establish a very effective feedback mechanism

**[00:32:40.980] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000359)  
where they will commit to accountability and responsiveness to the MTRCB

**[00:32:47.660] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000360)  
for when complaints are raised and expressed to the said online streaming provider,

**[00:32:55.880] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000361)  
their share.

**[00:32:57.060] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000362)  
So, there should be an answer.

**[00:32:59.280] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000363)  
There should be a commitment that they will work on these concerns

**[00:33:03.280] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000364)  
and that they're very much willing to help the MTRCB.

**[00:33:06.720] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000365)  
Currently, we are working with Netflix and HBO and Disney,

**[00:33:11.200] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000366)  
and they've been very cooperative.

**[00:33:13.180] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000367)  
I'm happy to share, Mr. Chair.

**[00:33:14.820] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000368)  
However, with the rampant problems that we are facing today,

**[00:33:19.920] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000369)  
I think it is faced globally.

**[00:33:22.800] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000370)  
We really need to reiterate that this regulation,

**[00:33:27.060] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000371)  
as possible,

**[00:33:28.460] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000372)  
but we would need their cooperation and their coordination as well.

**[00:33:33.900] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000373)  
All right.

**[00:33:35.020] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000374)  
Because my concern, ma'am,

**[00:33:36.700] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000375)  
is the growing violence in schools right now

**[00:33:44.540] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000376)  
because kids are watching online games,

**[00:33:48.300] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000377)  
yung access po nila sa mga online games.

**[00:33:53.000] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000378)  
Do you have any steps to address this?

**[00:33:55.800] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000379)  
Kaya pa paano?

**[00:33:56.400] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000380)  
Though you don't have these powers yet,

**[00:33:59.680] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000381)  
which are being linked to violent online games and violent content,

**[00:34:04.180] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000382)  
yung nangyari po sa Samwanga,

**[00:34:05.800] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000383)  
I mean, isang online game po yun na nire-record pa nung bata yung shootout na yun,

**[00:34:14.600] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000384)  
may steps po ba kayo?

**[00:34:16.400] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000385)  
Are you already talking about this?

**[00:34:18.740] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000386)  
How do you, your agency, will control this?

**[00:34:21.500] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000387)  
And I don't know if OMB can also help us on this matter.

**[00:34:25.240] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000388)  
Sure.

**[00:34:26.400] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000389)  
Currently, Mr. Chairman,

**[00:34:27.560] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000390)  
it is not under the purview of the MTRCB

**[00:34:30.140] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000391)  
with regard to online gaming and video games

**[00:34:33.380] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000392)  
as it is a totally different structure from what we are currently doing.

**[00:34:39.160] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000393)  
But we have explored

**[00:34:40.460] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000394)  
and we are continuing the discussions on this exploration.

**[00:34:44.380] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000395)  
And I would like to yield to the Executive Director of the MTRCB

**[00:34:48.040] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000396)  
for the reporting of the exploratory researches we've made.

**[00:34:54.560] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000397)  
Thank you.

**[00:34:56.400] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000398)  
Senator,

**[00:34:57.640] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000399)  
as regards violence in social media,

**[00:35:00.200] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000400)  
while it is a different area,

**[00:35:06.080] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000401)  
because MTRCB, as we are saying,

**[00:35:09.680] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000402)  
we are involved only in sa content, no?

**[00:35:12.620] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000403)  
And unfortunately, online contents,

**[00:35:15.300] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000404)  
hindi pa rin sa amin.

**[00:35:16.760] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000405)  
But there are studies globally

**[00:35:20.160] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000406)  
regarding this violence in media, in social media.

**[00:35:23.620] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000407)  
One of them is the,

**[00:35:25.500] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000408)  
do not amplify regulatory principle for social media,

**[00:35:30.800] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000409)  
which means we can,

**[00:35:34.580] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000410)  
as a country,

**[00:35:37.740] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000411)  
can create policies that require major platform

**[00:35:40.420] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000412)  
to address algorithmic amplification of violent content,

**[00:35:45.260] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000413)  
recommendation of increasingly extreme material,

**[00:35:49.080] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000414)  
autoplay of violent videos,

**[00:35:51.620] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000415)  
viral circulation of graphic violence,

**[00:35:53.840] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000416)  
monetization of violent content,

**[00:35:55.340] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000417)  
and recommendation of violent material to minors.

**[00:36:00.580] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000418)  
If there will be a law

**[00:36:03.480] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000419)  
na hahawakan ang iyong mga social media na ito,

**[00:36:08.240] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000420)  
we can tell them na dapat hindi naka-autoplay ang mga violent content

**[00:36:14.560] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000421)  
sa mga materials natin.

**[00:36:16.460] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000422)  
Dapat hindi, kung pwede, hindi ma-monetize.

**[00:36:21.180] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000423)  
Because sa totoo lang,

**[00:36:23.460] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000424)  
isa sa mga,

**[00:36:25.340] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000425)  
sa mga motivation ng mga content creators,

**[00:36:27.780] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000426)  
syempre, mamamonetize yung gagawin nilang content.

**[00:36:31.340] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000427)  
If these videos, at yung mga violent videos,

**[00:36:35.220] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000428)  
sex videos, ay made-demonetize,

**[00:36:39.060] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000429)  
I think, mababawasan yung mga ganong materials.

**[00:36:42.940] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000430)  
And then, yung algorithmic amplification,

**[00:36:46.940] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000431)  
we can tell Meta or other social media platforms na

**[00:36:52.860] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000432)  
automatic, walang algorithmic amplification,

**[00:36:55.180] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000433)  
automatic walang recommendations,

**[00:36:59.180] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000434)  
especially sa minors.

**[00:37:00.820] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000435)  
Automatic block sa minors, yung mga ganon.

**[00:37:04.020] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000436)  
We can also introduce a stronger protection for minors

**[00:37:08.880] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000437)  
by safety by default for minors principle.

**[00:37:14.500] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000438)  
Yun nga po, as I said, a while ago,

**[00:37:17.360] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000439)  
violent content should not be algorithmically recommended,

**[00:37:21.700] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000440)  
graphic content should be age-gated.

**[00:37:24.300] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000441)  
Autoplay.

**[00:37:25.020] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000442)  
violent content should be

**[00:37:27.020] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000443)  
disabled. Accounts should

**[00:37:29.100] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000444)  
have stronger privacy settings.

**[00:37:31.580] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000445)  
Live streaming should have enhanced

**[00:37:33.280] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000446)  
safeguards. Repeated

**[00:37:35.020] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000447)  
exposure to violent content should trigger

**[00:37:37.000] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000448)  
additional safeguards.

**[00:37:39.860] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000449)  
There are also studies

**[00:37:41.220] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000450)  
about or push for

**[00:37:42.660] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000451)  
creation of violent content

**[00:37:45.440] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000452)  
emergency protocols

**[00:37:46.860] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000453)  
in some countries.

**[00:37:48.960] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000454)  
This would be particularly

**[00:37:50.680] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000455)  
useful in the Philippines for events

**[00:37:53.000] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000456)  
such as mass shootings,

**[00:37:54.200] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000457)  
terrorist attacks, hostage situations,

**[00:37:57.360] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000458)  
riots, and

**[00:37:58.400] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000459)  
other

**[00:37:58.940] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000460)  
violent contents or

**[00:38:02.280] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000461)  
incidents. Platforms

**[00:38:04.420] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000462)  
should have emergency escalation mechanisms

**[00:38:06.720] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000463)  
with government

**[00:38:08.060] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000464)  
authorities.

**[00:38:11.460] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000465)  
We should treat live streaming

**[00:38:13.940] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000466)  
differently from ordinary posts

**[00:38:16.120] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000467)  
because

**[00:38:17.660] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000468)  
the harm happens before

**[00:38:20.160] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000469)  
traditional moderation

**[00:38:21.440] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000470)  
can respond.

**[00:38:23.700] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000471)  
Pwede mangyari.

**[00:38:24.200] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000472)  
May mangyaring hindi maganda kaya

**[00:38:26.100] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000473)  
we should treat live streaming

**[00:38:28.080] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000474)  
differently from regular

**[00:38:29.820] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000475)  
video posting.

**[00:38:33.200] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000476)  
Again,

**[00:38:34.860] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000477)  
yung pong established

**[00:38:36.740] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000478)  
platform accountability

**[00:38:38.680] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000479)  
kailangan po natin

**[00:38:41.280] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000480)  
i-implement

**[00:38:43.040] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000481)  
or pwedeng gawing policy.

**[00:38:44.960] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000482)  
So this is your study.

**[00:38:46.940] MTRCB Executive Director Roberto Diciembre** (asr-000483)  
Understudy po ito.

**[00:38:48.500] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000484)  
Unless you will be given

**[00:38:50.400] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000485)  
by Congress, by

**[00:38:52.120] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000486)  
Congress and the Senate,

**[00:38:54.200] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000487)  
to expand your powers

**[00:38:56.380] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000488)  
to look into

**[00:38:57.700] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000489)  
online platforms,

**[00:39:00.440] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000490)  
social media, and online games.

**[00:39:02.260] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000491)  
For now, wala ako kayo magagawa.

**[00:39:04.080] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000492)  
Yes. Alright. Thank you.

**[00:39:05.920] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000493)  
Moving forward, Chairperson.

**[00:39:08.860] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000494)  
Ano po ang

**[00:39:09.640] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000495)  
ginagawa ng MTRCB right now

**[00:39:16.380] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000496)  
dito po sa exhibition ng materials

**[00:39:18.300] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000497)  
ng mga unregistered

**[00:39:20.340] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000498)  
entities considering

**[00:39:21.820] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000499)  
the issue.

**[00:39:22.600] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000500)  
So,

**[00:39:24.200] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000501)  
you look through logik

**[00:39:26.120] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000502)  
ng mga

**[00:39:27.500] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000503)  
unregistered entities

**[00:39:28.940] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000504)  
accepting movies

**[00:39:30.160] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000505)  
without

**[00:39:30.480] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000506)  
a

**[00:39:30.600] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000507)  
limit.

**[00:39:35.060] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000508)  
The

**[00:39:35.840] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000509)  
Committee of the

**[00:39:38.800] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000510)  
MTRCB

**[00:39:39.820] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000511)  
right now.

**[00:39:42.440] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000512)  
And the Chairperson of the

**[00:39:44.320] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000513)  
Committee is with me now.

**[00:39:46.420] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000514)  
However, I'd like to answer the question

**[00:39:48.100] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000515)  
first.

**[00:39:49.820] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000516)  
The MTRCB has its own monitoring

**[00:39:51.980] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000517)  
inspection unit along with our

**[00:39:54.180] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000518)  
deputies and the board members who go around the different cinemas and we do our monitoring

**[00:39:58.320] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000519)  
inspection consistently. That helps us identify the violations or the violators,

**[00:40:06.660] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000520)  
especially in the cinemas. But we also do our own inspections around the country wherein we go

**[00:40:13.340] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000521)  
to the different communities and identify the unregistered cable companies as well.

**[00:40:18.620] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000522)  
Okay. Sa non-display or innocent display permit to exhibit,

**[00:40:27.620] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000523)  
what are the common violations encountered during monitoring and inspections?

**[00:40:32.360] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000524)  
Unregistered cable companies, Mr. Chair, fake, tampered permits to exhibit.

**[00:40:41.260] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000525)  
Okay. Isa pa ho yung medyo kontrobersyal na nai-palabas na po sa online. Ito pong

**[00:40:48.060] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000526)  
Lost Sabungeros. Does the MTRCB have the authority to refuse to review a film because

**[00:40:54.360] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000527)  
its subject matter is allegedly subjudice? Or is the board's mandate precisely to review the material,

**[00:41:01.900] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000528)  
determine whether it violates the law, and then give it to the appropriate classification?

**[00:41:06.720] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000529)  
Because if you refuse to review the film in the first place, aren't you preventing the producer

**[00:41:13.560] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000530)  
from exercising the very appeal process?

**[00:41:18.060] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000531)  
So, what are the policies that the MTRCB supposed to provide?

**[00:41:23.400] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000532)  
Mr. Chairman, first of all, yes we are in the process of reviewing our reassessing our processes.

**[00:41:31.360] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000533)  
However, I just want to clear this up. That regarding the Lost Sabungeros movie,

**[00:41:37.820] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000534)  
the board consistently reviews each and every material that is submitted to us carefully.

**[00:41:45.900] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000535)  
And it would not be detrimental to...

**[00:41:48.060] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000536)  
nor a disservice to the Filipino viewers to ask for additional requirements consistent with our subjudice rule

**[00:41:56.260] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000537)  
because this is only an exercise of prudence for an administrative agency that is a regulatory body as well.

**[00:42:06.280] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000538)  
Okay. Dito naman po tayo moving sa live television shows.

**[00:42:11.920] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000539)  
Following the recent It's Showtime controversy yung memory mar segment,

**[00:42:16.360] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000540)  
what concrete measures po is MTRCB implementing to prevent violations during live broadcast para hindi na po maulit din ito sa ibang mga program

**[00:42:28.440] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000541)  
rather than simply penalizing programs after the fact?

**[00:42:32.740] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000542)  
With regard to Showtime and this question, Mr. Chairman, I would yield to the Chairman of the Committee on Adjudication, Attorney Jojo Salomon.

**[00:42:41.600] MTRCB Board Member Antonio Ricardo ?Jojo? Salomon Jr.** (asr-000543)  
Good morning, Senator.

**[00:42:44.600] MTRCB Board Member Antonio Ricardo ?Jojo? Salomon Jr.** (asr-000544)  
As regards to the memory mar segment,

**[00:42:46.360] MTRCB Board Member Antonio Ricardo ?Jojo? Salomon Jr.** (asr-000545)  
we called the production for an adjugatory hearing.

**[00:42:53.500] MTRCB Board Member Antonio Ricardo ?Jojo? Salomon Jr.** (asr-000546)  
And in that hearing, we discussed how they can prevent this from happening again.

**[00:42:58.980] MTRCB Board Member Antonio Ricardo ?Jojo? Salomon Jr.** (asr-000547)  
And in so doing, we required them to assign a compliance officer who would monitor their program as it airs.

**[00:43:08.460] MTRCB Board Member Antonio Ricardo ?Jojo? Salomon Jr.** (asr-000548)  
So this compliance officer will be the one responsible to the MTRCB.

**[00:43:16.360] MTRCB Board Member Antonio Ricardo ?Jojo? Salomon Jr.** (asr-000549)  
In case of live violations.

**[00:43:20.080] MTRCB Board Member Antonio Ricardo ?Jojo? Salomon Jr.** (asr-000550)  
And also, we required them to undergo a compliance seminar,

**[00:43:24.100] MTRCB Board Member Antonio Ricardo ?Jojo? Salomon Jr.** (asr-000551)  
which we do to all violators of MTRCB rules.

**[00:45:19.820] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000593)  
I mean, yung tao, siyempre, nakikisama, natutuwa din pero nagiging tuksuhan, finufocus, pilay, bulag, duling, bingi, itim, etc., etc. Parang nilalait yung tao. What is your take on that?

**[00:45:37.320] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000594)

**[00:46:19.100] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000597)  
that they are used to because they do it every day.

**[00:46:22.320] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000598)

**[00:46:39.400] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000599)

**[00:47:05.880] MTRCB Chairperson Diorella Maria ?Lala? Sotto-Antonio** (asr-000603)

**[00:47:19.100] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000604)

**[00:47:49.100] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000607)

**[00:55:40.320] OMB OIC Chairperson Dennis Lorenzo B. Pinlac** (asr-000687)  
[unclear] We will be presenting a short briefing on what transpired during the Optical Media Board. Okay, first slide please. As we all know, the Optical Media Board was created under Republic Act 9239, in which the OMB is mandated to promote the protection of intellectual property rights, specifically copyright, by regulating the mastering, manufacturing, importing, exporting, distribution and selling of optical and magnetic media.

**[00:56:10.320] OMB OIC Chairperson Dennis Lorenzo B. Pinlac** (asr-000688)  
When we say magnetic media, ito rin po yung mga, ano, yung mga hard disk drive na nasa computer natin, as well as its technical variations thereof. Okay. The vision of the Optical Media Board is an economy which is free from optical media piracy, wherein there is a level playing field for all legitimate players. Of course, our mission is to promote and protect intellectual property in digital form.

**[00:56:40.320] OMB OIC Chairperson Dennis Lorenzo B. Pinlac** (asr-000689)  
At present, the OMB?s mandate is sixfold. First is the permits and licenses. Second, we are also empowered to conduct proactive inspections, and to act as complainant, and to hear and resolve administrative cases, and create and maintain a database of licensed media and establishments, as well as establish support offices when necessary. For our fiscal performance as of last year,

**[00:57:10.320] OMB OIC Chairperson Dennis Lorenzo B. Pinlac** (asr-000690)  
for the allotment received, we have a grand total of 73,302,000. Obligations incurred were 56,280,000. Disbursements were 53,749,000, wherein the percentage of obligations is 76.78% and the percentage of disbursement is 95.50%.

**[00:57:40.320] OMB OIC Chairperson Dennis Lorenzo B. Pinlac** (asr-000691)  
For fiscal performance of this year as of June 2026,

**[00:57:45.160] OMB OIC Chairperson Dennis Lorenzo B. Pinlac** (asr-000692)  
for allotment, we have received P53,681,000.

**[00:57:49.660] OMB OIC Chairperson Dennis Lorenzo B. Pinlac** (asr-000693)  
Our obligations incurred is P24,277,000.

**[00:57:55.180] OMB OIC Chairperson Dennis Lorenzo B. Pinlac** (asr-000694)  
And the disbursements is P23,830,000.

**[00:57:59.580] OMB OIC Chairperson Dennis Lorenzo B. Pinlac** (asr-000695)  
Of these, the percentage of obligations is 45.28%

**[00:58:05.340] OMB OIC Chairperson Dennis Lorenzo B. Pinlac** (asr-000696)  
and the percentage of disbursement is 98.16%.

**[00:58:10.320] OMB OIC Chairperson Dennis Lorenzo B. Pinlac** (asr-000697)  
For our financial performance as of December of 2025,

**[00:58:18.140] OMB OIC Chairperson Dennis Lorenzo B. Pinlac** (asr-000698)  
as seen in the graph,

**[00:58:19.300] OMB OIC Chairperson Dennis Lorenzo B. Pinlac** (asr-000699)  
we have received a total revenue of P52,060,911.

**[00:58:28.540] OMB OIC Chairperson Dennis Lorenzo B. Pinlac** (asr-000700)  
This is an increase and for the record,

**[00:58:33.400] OMB OIC Chairperson Dennis Lorenzo B. Pinlac** (asr-000701)

**[01:11:29.580] OMB Administrative Chief Janine Joyce G. Sison** (asr-000844)

**[01:11:38.580] OMB Administrative Chief Janine Joyce G. Sison** (asr-000847)  
media board under the FY 2026.

**[01:11:43.580] OMB Administrative Chief Janine Joyce G. Sison** (asr-000848)

**[01:11:47.580] OMB Administrative Chief Janine Joyce G. Sison** (asr-000849)  
This provision has placed the agency in extraordinary operational and legal Limbo.

**[01:11:56.580] OMB Administrative Chief Janine Joyce G. Sison** (asr-000850)  
While the special provisions directs the winding down of the agency,

**[01:11:59.580] OMB Administrative Chief Janine Joyce G. Sison** (asr-000851)  
The Republic Act No. 9239 remains substantive, unrepealed law. The pending abolition measures, Senate Bills 991 and 1654, are still pending in the legislative mill. As demonstrated by our frontline data, piracy and illicit hardware importations have not disappeared. They have modernized. In 2026 alone, our team conducted over 1,850 inspections and collected nearly 30 million in revenue.

**[01:12:29.580] OMB Administrative Chief Janine Joyce G. Sison** (asr-000852)  
As brought to light in our May and June 2026 multi-agency consultations both with DBM and CSE, full implementation of personnel placement and asset turnover is on hold, awaiting clear executive guidance.

**[01:12:45.260] OMB Administrative Chief Janine Joyce G. Sison** (asr-000853)  
Until the formal issuance of the executive order or the legislative resolution on the pending bills, the OMB must be granted adequate operational flexibility and budget headroom.

**[01:12:57.260] OMB Administrative Chief Janine Joyce G. Sison** (asr-000854)  
Mr. Chair, we earnestly manifest...

**[01:12:59.580] OMB Administrative Chief Janine Joyce G. Sison** (asr-000855)  
...critical need for a proper, dignified, and seamless transition of our personnel.

**[01:13:04.580] OMB Administrative Chief Janine Joyce G. Sison** (asr-000856)

**[01:13:14.580] OMB Administrative Chief Janine Joyce G. Sison** (asr-000857)  
These are dedicated civil servants who continue to exceed performance targets, process thousands of licenses, despite the looming uncertainty of our livelihoods.

**[01:13:24.580] OMB Administrative Chief Janine Joyce G. Sison** (asr-000858)  
We appeal to this honorable committee to support in ensuring AUM's integrity.

**[01:13:29.580] OMB Administrative Chief Janine Joyce G. Sison** (asr-000861)  
Our 2016 but concerned main transition plan safeguards our welfare and career security before budgetary or operational phaseout is finalized.

**[01:13:39.540] OMB Administrative Chief Janine Joyce G. Sison** (asr-000863)  
Mr. Chair, thank you so much for your time, guidance and continued support of our agency.

**[01:13:43.540] OMB Administrative Chief Janine Joyce G. Sison** (asr-000864)

**[01:35:50.220] FDCP Executive Director Rachelle Villaluna** (asr-001277)

**[01:35:54.080] FDCP Executive Director Rachelle Villaluna** (asr-001278)  
ratio meaning that...

**[01:35:55.320] FDCP Executive Director Rachelle Villaluna** (asr-001279)  
for the incentives that we are providing for the Film Location Incentive Program,

**[01:36:02.620] FDCP Executive Director Rachelle Villaluna** (asr-001280)  
there is a 1 is to 13 economic return ratio,

**[01:36:06.220] FDCP Executive Director Rachelle Villaluna** (asr-001281)  
which means for every 1 peso grant that we are providing, 13 pesos goes back to the economy.

**[01:36:10.880] FDCP Executive Director Rachelle Villaluna** (asr-001282)  
Those projects are purely foreign projects that are shooting here in the Philippines.

**[01:36:15.920] FDCP Executive Director Rachelle Villaluna** (asr-001283)  
And then for the projects that are co-produced by Filipino producers,

**[01:36:19.560] FDCP Executive Director Rachelle Villaluna** (asr-001284)  
the economic return ratio is 1 is to 16, meaning for every 1 peso that we provide as grant,

**[01:36:25.320] FDCP Executive Director Rachelle Villaluna** (asr-001285)  
16 pesos goes back to the economy.

**[01:36:28.140] FDCP Executive Director Rachelle Villaluna** (asr-001286)  
And then for the local production grant that we are providing,

**[01:36:31.800] FDCP Executive Director Rachelle Villaluna** (asr-001287)  
there is a 1 is to 6 economic return ratio,

**[01:36:34.680] FDCP Executive Director Rachelle Villaluna** (asr-001288)  
meaning 1 peso grant, 6 pesos goes back to the economy.

**[01:36:39.260] FDCP Executive Director Rachelle Villaluna** (asr-001289)  
So the local film production really has benefits in terms of our economic growth.

**[01:36:48.000] FDCP Executive Director Rachelle Villaluna** (asr-001290)

**[01:42:06.080] FDCP Executive Director Rachelle Villaluna** (asr-001361)  
to have access to discussions among key personalities in the industry

**[01:42:13.080] FDCP Executive Director Rachelle Villaluna** (asr-001362)  
or pillars of the industry.

**[01:42:15.080] FDCP Executive Director Rachelle Villaluna** (asr-001363)  
Next is a 1.5 million request for the procurement of the humidifiers

**[01:42:21.080] FDCP Executive Director Rachelle Villaluna** (asr-001364)  
for Cinemathèque centers.

**[01:42:23.080] FDCP Executive Director Rachelle Villaluna** (asr-001365)  
It's because the building design requires us to have the humidifiers

**[01:42:30.080] FDCP Executive Director Rachelle Villaluna** (asr-001366)  
because right now because of its design,

**[01:42:33.080] FDCP Executive Director Rachelle Villaluna** (asr-001367)  
it grows a lot of molds whenever we have this rainy season.

**[01:42:38.080] FDCP Executive Director Rachelle Villaluna** (asr-001368)  
So we are also requesting additional 1.5 million for that.

**[01:42:43.080] FDCP Executive Director Rachelle Villaluna** (asr-001369)  
So a total of 8.7 million for the GMS or the General Management Service.

**[01:42:48.080] FDCP Executive Director Rachelle Villaluna** (asr-001370)  
Total breakdown for the wishlist that we have is 311 million pesos.

**[01:43:00.080] FDCP Executive Director Rachelle Villaluna** (asr-001371)  
And I would like to pass on the floor to our chairman.

**[01:43:04.080] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001372)  
Just some points of clarification why the Cinemathèque

**[01:43:07.080] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001373)  
Oh, I'm sorry.

**[01:43:09.080] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001374)  
Just a point of clarification why the Cinemathèque.

**[01:43:12.080] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001375)  
The Cinemathèque are micro cinemas which are located in various provinces.

**[01:43:17.080] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001376)  
There is one in Bacolo, there is one in Iloilo, and there is one in Davao.

**[01:43:22.080] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001377)  
But then we hopefully will be able to build more Cinemathèque before 2028

**[01:43:28.080] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001378)  
when this administration would have to step down

**[01:43:31.080] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001379)  
being coterminous with the present administration.

**[01:43:34.080] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001380)  
Why the Cinemathèque?

**[01:43:35.080] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001381)  
Because these are not only venues for showing films

**[01:43:38.080] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001382)  
but it has become also a cultural hub

**[01:43:40.080] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001383)  
for regional filmmakers to get together

**[01:43:42.080] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001384)  
and have their activities in these various venues.

**[01:43:45.080] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001385)  
So it's almost an imperative, it's almost a need

**[01:43:48.080] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001386)  
for, you know, to provide the venue for regional filmmakers

**[01:43:56.080] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001387)  
as well as cinemathèque

**[01:44:00.080] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001388)  
to see the problem

**[01:44:10.080] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001389)  
to see the problem of digitalization.

**[01:44:16.080] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001390)  
I've done over 100 films as a director

**[01:44:19.080] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001391)  
and I've come to realize that maybe 80% of them are already dead films.

**[01:44:24.080] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001392)  
Because if you allow these, especially those shot in cells as negatives

**[01:44:30.080] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001393)  
the level of deterioration is very quick.

**[01:44:33.080] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001394)  
Now, unless we are able to preserve these, to digitize these

**[01:44:37.080] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001395)  
and preserve these immediately

**[01:44:39.080] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001396)  
we might even lose more films.

**[01:44:42.080] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001397)  
What is so sad is some of the masterpieces we have restored

**[01:44:47.080] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001398)  
were not recovered here in our country but abroad.

**[01:44:50.080] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001399)  
So these are some major priorities.

**[01:44:53.080] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001400)  
We also have a priority, Mr. Chairman, in terms of incentives.

**[01:44:57.080] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001401)  
Mapakatotoo na tayo na hindi natin kayang pantayan ang incentives

**[01:45:00.000] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001402)  
na binibigay ng ibang bansa dahil sila ay mas mayaman

**[01:45:03.920] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001403)  
at ang gobyerno nila ay mas may maluwag na magbigay ng pera

**[01:45:07.680] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001404)  
para mahingkayat ang mga tao na mag-shoot sa kanila.

**[01:45:11.480] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001405)  
Ang ating tanging panlawan lamang ay magagaling ang mga Pilipino

**[01:45:15.340] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001406)  
sa kanilang ginagawa at tayo ay nagsasalita ng wikang Ingles.

**[01:45:19.420] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001407)  
Kaya mas madali para sa foreigners na pumunta dito.

**[01:45:22.780] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001408)  
Ang laki-laking economic benefit kapag nagsushoot dito ang mga foreigners.

**[01:45:27.880] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001409)  
Why? Number one, hindi lamang yung mga tao sa pelikula ang kumikita.

**[01:45:32.440] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001410)  
May exchange of technology.

**[01:45:34.200] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001411)  
At ikalawa, ang daming ancillary businesses na nakikinabang

**[01:45:37.920] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001412)  
tuwing may nagsushooting dito.

**[01:45:41.220] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001413)  
Wishful thinking ito na sana mas matulungan at sana mas madagdagan

**[01:45:44.820] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001414)  
ang incentives na magbibigay natin.

**[01:45:46.500] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001415)  
But we're also being very realistic in terms of the priorities of the government

**[01:45:50.300] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001416)  
as to where they would put the tax pay.

**[01:45:52.080] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001417)  
Mr. Chairman, let me cut in.

**[01:45:56.640] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001418)  
Could it be?

**[01:45:57.880] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001419)  
Could it be na incentives lang kaya hindi tayo makapaghikay?

**[01:46:01.780] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001420)  
Or there may be other reasons.

**[01:46:04.000] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001421)  
Are you also looking to the fact na maaring peace and order

**[01:46:08.100] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001422)  
kaya ayaw pumunta dito ng mga artista or political turmoil

**[01:46:12.400] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001423)  
na medyo turn off para sa kanila, na medyo magulo tayo dito,

**[01:46:17.640] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001424)  
nag-aaway, may mga kinasuhan ng mga opisyal ng gobyerno.

**[01:46:22.620] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001425)  
Couldn't be some of those are some of the reasons kung bakit

**[01:46:26.320] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001426)  
na turn off sila.

**[01:46:27.880] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001427)  
Bakit ako magsushoot dyan?

**[01:46:29.460] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001428)  
Rally dito, rally doon, sigawan dito, yung vicepresidente nila nandoon,

**[01:46:34.140] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001429)  
kinakasuhan, yung mga opisyal nila nakakulong, ilang mga miyembro na senado,

**[01:46:40.180] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001430)  
et cetera, or I would siguro prefer a place na kamukha na Pilipinas pero tahimik,

**[01:46:46.520] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001431)  
like Thailand, tahimik siya.

**[01:46:48.640] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001432)  
E dito lang tayo kaysa baka mapunta tayo, madamay tayo sa gulo.

**[01:46:52.040] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001433)  
Hindi niyo ba naisip yan na maaring hindi lang doon sa incentive?

**[01:46:57.000] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001434)  
Ano?

**[01:46:57.480] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001435)  
Naka-factor in na rin po yun.

**[01:46:59.040] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001436)  
Naka-factor in po yun dahil ang perception ng mundo sa Pilipinas,

**[01:47:04.140] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001437)  
lalo-lalo na sa ngayon at kung ano-ano ang lumalabas sa balita

**[01:47:07.260] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001438)  
at hinahighlight ng balita ay isang factor yan.

**[01:47:10.840] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001439)  
Pero ang napaka katakataka, hindi naman katakataka,

**[01:47:14.160] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001440)  
ang lalo nakakalakas ng loob, Mr. Chair,

**[01:47:17.240] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001441)  
is na dito pa rin sila pumupunta.

**[01:47:19.420] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001442)  
Okay, in spite and despite, dito pa rin sila pumupunta.

**[01:47:22.100] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001443)  
Pero hindi po natin mapapagkaila na ang perception nila ng Pilipinas

**[01:47:26.200] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001444)  
ay a very big factor in not only in the movie industry

**[01:47:29.620] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001445)  
but the entire business setup.

**[01:47:32.080] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001446)  
Okay, naka-factor in po yan.

**[01:47:34.140] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001447)  
Okay.

**[01:47:35.280] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001448)  
Okay, my next question will be

**[01:47:39.820] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001449)  
yung hinihingi po ninyong proposed budget na 425 million, ano po,

**[01:47:47.140] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001450)  
pero 314 million lamang nasa NEP.

**[01:47:50.980] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001451)  
At meron pa kayong 311 million na sa wish list.

**[01:47:54.800] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001452)  
Alright, so,

**[01:47:55.320] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001453)  
if kukumputin ho ninyo yan,

**[01:47:58.060] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001454)  
parang bakit po umahabot sa 625 million

**[01:48:01.360] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001455)  
ang inyong total funding requirement

**[01:48:03.260] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001456)  
kung 425 million lamang ang inyong original proposal, Ms. Rachel?

**[01:48:08.760] FDCP Executive Director Rachelle Villaluna** (asr-001457)  
Actually po, one is for the film restoration.

**[01:48:13.540] FDCP Executive Director Rachelle Villaluna** (asr-001458)  
The team who's handling the restoration has submitted us their proposal

**[01:48:19.100] FDCP Executive Director Rachelle Villaluna** (asr-001459)  
which is amounting to 50 million.

**[01:48:21.300] FDCP Executive Director Rachelle Villaluna** (asr-001460)  
Just recently, when they were able to identify,

**[01:48:25.320] FDCP Executive Director Rachelle Villaluna** (asr-001461)  
the titles.

**[01:48:26.460] FDCP Executive Director Rachelle Villaluna** (asr-001462)  
And that was even before the budget call of the DBM

**[01:48:30.420] FDCP Executive Director Rachelle Villaluna** (asr-001463)  
was conducted early January or January this year.

**[01:48:35.800] FDCP Executive Director Rachelle Villaluna** (asr-001464)  
And then for the additional budget, again,

**[01:48:39.260] FDCP Executive Director Rachelle Villaluna** (asr-001465)  
is from the incentives

**[01:48:40.640] FDCP Executive Director Rachelle Villaluna** (asr-001466)  
because the cycle 2 opened last September

**[01:48:44.420] FDCP Executive Director Rachelle Villaluna** (asr-001467)  
and several film productions,

**[01:48:47.580] FDCP Executive Director Rachelle Villaluna** (asr-001468)  
both for foreign and local, have applied.

**[01:48:50.400] FDCP Executive Director Rachelle Villaluna** (asr-001469)  
And they're all good projects.

**[01:48:52.640] FDCP Executive Director Rachelle Villaluna** (asr-001470)  
And based on their application,

**[01:48:55.320] FDCP Executive Director Rachelle Villaluna** (asr-001471)  
again, they are spending a lot here in the country.

**[01:48:59.760] FDCP Executive Director Rachelle Villaluna** (asr-001472)  
So, we're just wondering if we can give more

**[01:49:04.260] FDCP Executive Director Rachelle Villaluna** (asr-001473)  
or we can accept more applications

**[01:49:08.860] FDCP Executive Director Rachelle Villaluna** (asr-001474)  
and approve their applications for the incentives

**[01:49:12.120] FDCP Executive Director Rachelle Villaluna** (asr-001475)  
because they might look for another country

**[01:49:15.820] FDCP Executive Director Rachelle Villaluna** (asr-001476)  
or alternative country if we don't give that incentives to them.

**[01:49:20.440] FDCP Executive Director Rachelle Villaluna** (asr-001477)  
We just finalized the deliberation last month.

**[01:49:24.320] FDCP Executive Director Rachelle Villaluna** (asr-001478)  
So,

**[01:49:25.320] FDCP Executive Director Rachelle Villaluna** (asr-001479)  
those budgets are factored in in our wish list that was presented earlier.

**[01:49:31.600] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001480)  
If I can add to that, Mr. Chair,

**[01:49:33.520] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001481)  
kasalanan ko yung isa yun sa preservation

**[01:49:35.700] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001482)  
kasi pinahanap ko kung ano pa yung mga magagandang pelikula

**[01:49:39.320] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001483)  
na maa-access natin na pwedeng ma-restore.

**[01:49:42.180] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001484)  
And it takes time to find all these

**[01:49:43.780] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001485)  
until we came up with a list na,

**[01:49:45.160] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001486)  
ay, ito pa lang, restorable.

**[01:49:46.660] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001487)  
Kaya hindi na-factor in yun sa una.

**[01:49:48.360] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001488)  
At sa kakalawa, maganda po yung problema na

**[01:49:50.440] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001489)  
ang dami-dami nag-a-apply

**[01:49:51.840] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001490)  
na kumuha ng mga grants and incentives

**[01:49:54.880] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001491)  
to be able to shoot here in the country

**[01:49:57.000] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001492)  
and at the same time

**[01:49:57.920] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001493)  
to have Philippine film workers

**[01:50:00.560] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001494)  
join in in these productions.

**[01:50:03.120] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001495)  
So, it's a good problem

**[01:50:04.440] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001496)  
which eventually became a budgetary problem.

**[01:50:06.760] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001497)  
Going back to film restoration,

**[01:50:10.420] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001498)  
how does the FDCP ensure

**[01:50:12.780] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001499)  
that restoring a project or a film

**[01:50:16.560] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001500)  
is technically feasible and cost-effective

**[01:50:19.520] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001501)  
before public funds are allocated?

**[01:50:22.520] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001502)  
Baka di naman natin mabawi.

**[01:50:23.840] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001503)  
Baka wala nang,

**[01:50:24.880] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001504)  
yung mga interest,

**[01:50:25.560] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001505)  
yung mga black and white,

**[01:50:27.660] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001506)  
naging brown and white,

**[01:50:29.020] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001507)  
may manunood pa ba noon?

**[01:50:30.120] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001508)  
Hindi na kilala ng mga kabataan ngayon,

**[01:50:32.080] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001509)  
mga nangamatay na yung mga tao na yun.

**[01:50:34.680] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001510)  
And baka boring yung sa inyo,

**[01:50:37.760] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001511)  
mga film directors, of course.

**[01:50:39.420] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001512)  
Meron po kayong keen,

**[01:50:40.620] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001513)  
may eye po kayo na maganda ito.

**[01:50:42.240] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001514)  
Pero sa ordinary viewer,

**[01:50:44.500] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001515)  
lalo mga kabataan ngayon,

**[01:50:45.780] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001516)  
mga Gen Z, Gen X, Gen Y,

**[01:50:48.480] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001517)  
eh, hindi sa kanila ikang kaakit-akit.

**[01:50:54.000] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001518)  
I'll answer.

**[01:50:54.880] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001519)  
I'll answer that in two points, Mr. Chair.

**[01:50:56.480] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001520)  
Firstly, before we restore a film,

**[01:50:59.000] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001521)  
we make sure that the film is still restorable.

**[01:51:01.840] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001522)  
Okay?

**[01:51:02.200] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001523)  
We have technical people assessing

**[01:51:03.880] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001524)  
the state of the negative

**[01:51:05.040] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001525)  
or the state of the print

**[01:51:06.060] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001526)  
if it is still accessible

**[01:51:07.600] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001527)  
or it is still restorable.

**[01:51:11.260] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001528)  
There are certain cases in which

**[01:51:13.200] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001529)  
the film is just so damaged

**[01:51:15.380] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001530)  
that we don't pursue it anymore

**[01:51:17.140] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001531)  
because it's going to cause an arm and a leg

**[01:51:19.260] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001532)  
and your next reincarnation

**[01:51:20.700] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001533)  
in order to completely restore it.

**[01:51:22.780] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001534)  
Ikalawa,

**[01:51:23.500] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001535)  
about the new generation

**[01:51:25.200] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001536)  
appreciating old films.

**[01:51:26.480] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001537)  
I will use an example of what happened

**[01:51:28.060] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001538)  
last September 4

**[01:51:29.540] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001539)  
at the opening of the

**[01:51:30.660] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001540)  
Philippine Film Industry Month.

**[01:51:32.360] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001541)  
For the first time,

**[01:51:33.460] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001542)  
ang mga Gen Z at Gen Y and Gen X

**[01:51:36.360] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001543)  
napanood ang tinimbangka,

**[01:51:38.280] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001544)  
ngunit kulang.

**[01:51:39.340] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001545)  
At nagulat sila.

**[01:51:40.960] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001546)  
Pero, Mr. Chairman,

**[01:51:41.980] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001547)  
hindi kayo, baka mga yung nanood na yun

**[01:51:44.040] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001548)  
ay mga estudyante

**[01:51:46.060] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001549)  
na mga ang courses nila

**[01:51:47.540] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001550)  
ay nasa film.

**[01:51:48.660] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001551)  
Ay, ay, hindi po.

**[01:51:49.920] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001552)  
Hindi po.

**[01:51:50.520] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001553)  
It was a real cross-section of people.

**[01:51:52.560] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001554)  
And as a matter,

**[01:51:53.500] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001555)  
a real fact,

**[01:51:54.120] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001556)  
may mga request kami

**[01:51:55.160] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001557)  
to show the film commercially.

**[01:51:57.720] FDCP Chairman Jose Javier Reyes** (asr-001558)

kaming na-receive is 18 million na lang po.

**[02:08:56.840] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002088)  
We now proceed with the presentation of the proposed budget of the Philippine Racing Commission.

**[02:09:03.340] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002089)  
Chairman de Leon, please proceed with your presentation and thank you for patiently waiting.

**[02:09:09.400] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002090)  
Dr. Edwin, Mr. Chair, salamat sa opportunity for us to present our budget for 2027.

**[02:09:16.060] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002091)  
As we all know, Philippine Racing Commission created 52 years ago by Presidential Decree No. 420 by President Marcos Sr.

**[02:09:25.460] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002092)  
So, we are mandated to provide employment to the Philippine people and generate taxes for the government.

**[02:09:33.480] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002093)  
So far, we are providing more than 5,000 direct and indirect employment to the Philippine people.

**[02:09:40.680] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002094)  
And at the same time, we are also producing OFW to Japan, Australia, and other parts of the country.

**[02:09:47.140] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002095)  
As of now, there are more than 2,000 OFW in Japan alone.

**[02:09:52.800] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002096)  
That came from the Philippine horse racing industry.

**[02:09:56.180] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002097)  
So, we are mandated to provide government revenue every year.

**[02:10:01.960] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002098)  
At the average, for the last five years, we are providing 700 million to 800 million per year to the government.

**[02:10:09.680] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002099)  
The budget that is being given to us by the government is only one-fifth of the total revenue that we are giving.

**[02:10:17.900] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002100)  
If they give us 200 million, we are returning 800 million to the government.

**[02:10:23.180] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002101)  
So,

**[02:10:25.460] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002102)  
we are mandated to supervise the horse racing, at the same time, the breeding industry in the Philippines.

**[02:10:35.760] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002103)  
Horse racing in the Philippines started 159 years ago.

**[02:10:39.620] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002104)  
So, we are ahead of some other nations in Asia and worldwide.

**[02:10:46.400] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002105)  
This is the only industry or sports industry that is accepted by more than 140 countries worldwide.

**[02:10:54.380] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002106)  
So,

**[02:10:55.460] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002107)  
as we observed in the last five years when we traveled to other conferences in the Asian Racing Federation,

**[02:11:04.060] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002108)  
International Federation of Horse Racing Authority, and APAR,

**[02:11:09.640] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002109)  
we found out that horse racing is really the only sport or one of the very few sports in the Philippines that can be converted to a sports tourism industry.

**[02:11:22.800] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002110)  
So, all sectors,

**[02:11:24.260] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002111)  
you know,

**[02:11:24.760] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002112)  
IPO observed,

**[02:11:25.460] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002113)  
from the King of Saudi Arabia to the different royalties in Europe, are all into horse racing.

**[02:11:35.160] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002114)  
So, we are left behind, Mr. Chair, for so many years.

**[02:11:39.640] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002115)  
Napabayaan po ito ng mga nakalang administrasyon at hindi nakita yung potential nito.

**[02:11:45.300] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002116)  
To give you an example,

**[02:11:47.560] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002117)  
right now,

**[02:11:48.900] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002118)  
Japan,

**[02:11:50.500] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002119)  
in terms of peso sales per year,

**[02:11:52.840] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002120)  
is selling 1.3 trillion pesos per year.

**[02:11:54.520] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002121)  
1.3 trillion pesos per year.

**[02:11:55.460] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002122)  
While Korea, right now, is selling 700 billion pesos per year.

**[02:12:03.720] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002123)  
So, we are of the same number in terms of horses as of Korea.

**[02:12:09.700] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002124)  
If they are selling 700 billion pesos a year,

**[02:12:13.680] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002125)  
and we are just targeting 10 billion so far,

**[02:12:17.800] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002126)  
napakalayo po ng depensya.

**[02:12:19.060] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002127)  
May I intercut you there, Mr. Chairman?

**[02:12:35.500] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002130)

**[02:12:37.080] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002131)

**[02:12:54.180] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002141)

**[02:12:55.940] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002144)

**[02:12:57.140] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002145)  
Tama po.

**[02:12:57.600] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002146)  
Pagkatapos pagbayaran, nakasimangot pa yung kahera pag nanalo.

**[02:13:02.060] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002147)  
May tao po ako dati.

**[02:13:03.940] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002148)

**[02:13:05.420] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002149)  
Nagkakarera tuwing hapon.

**[02:13:07.580] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002150)  
Nagrereklamo dahil napakahaba pa ng pila.

**[02:13:10.100] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002151)  
Doon po, systematic.

**[02:13:12.160] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002152)  
Pag saksak mo ng card, pag nanalo, bayad sa iyo.

**[02:13:16.380] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002153)  
Dito.

**[02:13:16.900] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002154)

**[02:13:36.380] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002163)

**[02:14:10.300] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002176)
