**[02:14:11.800] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002177)

**[02:28:46.940] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002604)  
sobra-sobra, 9999,

**[02:28:48.940] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002605)

**[02:28:50.940] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002606)  
sa bawat piso.

**[02:28:52.940] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002607)  
So, na-address po ba ninyo ito?

**[02:28:54.940] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002608)

**[02:28:56.940] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002609)

**[02:28:58.940] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002610)  
Actually, sa amin po yan,

**[02:29:00.940] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002611)  
actually, every Monday po, ang karera po

**[02:29:02.940] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002612)  
kasi natin, Merkules, Bernays,

**[02:29:04.940] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002613)  
Sabado, Linggo. Every Monday po,

**[02:29:06.940] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002614)  
meron kami tinatawag na

**[02:29:08.940] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002615)  
Race Review Committee, the whole day po yun.

**[02:29:10.940] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002616)  
Lahat ng nakaraang apat na araw na karera,

**[02:29:12.940] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002617)  
nire-review po yan ng committee of

**[02:29:14.940] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002618)  
8 people. So, ando po

**[02:29:16.940] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002619)  
yung mga commissioners, ando po yung mga FID

**[02:29:18.940] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002620)  
personnel namin.

**[02:29:20.940] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002621)  
Lahat po nang hindi tinawagan

**[02:29:22.940] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002622)  
ng steward, kasi may mga steward po ang bawat club eh,

**[02:29:24.940] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002623)  
sa bawat karera sila tumatawag

**[02:29:26.940] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002624)  
para suspindihan. Mga hindi nila tinawag

**[02:29:28.940] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002625)  
o malintawag nila,

**[02:29:30.940] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002626)  
nire-review namin yan, nagmamotopropyo kami

**[02:29:32.940] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002627)  
para imbestigahan yun.

**[02:29:34.940] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002628)  
So, para, for the last

**[02:29:36.940] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002629)  
5 years,

**[02:29:38.940] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002630)  
nakapag-ban na po kami ng hinete,

**[02:29:40.940] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002631)  
nakapag-ban na po kami ng trainers,

**[02:29:42.940] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002632)  
nakapag-ban na po kami ng handicapper, at marami na po kami

**[02:29:44.940] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002633)  
na suspindi.

**[02:29:46.940] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002634)  
1 year, 6 months, so lahat po ng

**[02:29:48.940] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002635)  
violations, may corresponding suspension.

**[02:29:50.940] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002636)  
Ito ba ang dahilan kung bakit

**[02:29:52.940] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002637)  
marami tayong mga

**[02:29:54.940] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002638)  
jockeys na nasa abroad na

**[02:29:56.940] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002639)  
sumasakay sa Hong Kong, sumasakay po

**[02:29:58.940] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002640)  
sa Japan.

**[02:30:00.000] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002641)  
O sa Saudi, dahil suspendido sila dito sa atin?

**[02:30:05.500] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002642)  
Or mas malaki ang bayad sa kanila ang riding fee? Magkano na po nga po ang riding fee natin dito?

**[02:30:12.400] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002643)  
Nung nagsimula kami magmamuno ng komisyon, wala pong binibigay ang Philippine Racing Commission sa mga inete.

**[02:30:19.760] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002644)  
Nagde-depende sila sa mga owner ng kabayo para bigyan sila ng riding fee.

**[02:30:24.480] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002645)  
Ngayon po, actually ngayon, presently, nagbibigay kami ng 800 per ride.

**[02:30:30.000] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002646)  
Win or lose?

**[02:30:31.860] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002647)  
Win or lose.

**[02:30:32.660] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002648)  
Ngayon,

**[02:30:33.400] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002649)  
Nakaka-apat siya na ride, so 800 times 4.

**[02:30:36.500] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002650)  
Apo, ngayon, pagka-stake race po yan, pag 1 million ng premio, 1,000 per riding fee nila. Win or lose?

**[02:30:44.160] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002651)  
Meron po kami 15 million race, presidential gold cup, in the name of our president, 15,000 per ride doon.

**[02:30:51.040] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002652)  
At win or lose din po yun.

**[02:30:52.780] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002653)  
Ngayon, with our trust to digitalize our operation,

**[02:30:57.480] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002654)  
we ask all the jockeys to have their own,

**[02:31:00.000] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002655)  
ATM.

**[02:31:00.660] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002656)  
pagkating po ng Friday, nandun po sila sa ATM, kumukubra po sila doon before lunch.

**[02:31:06.280] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002657)  
So, the average income of the top 10 jockey today is,

**[02:31:12.360] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002658)  
yung 1 to 3 po, ma-average po sila 300 to 350,000 per month.

**[02:31:18.120] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002659)  
Yung nandun po sa 7, 8, 9, 10, 150 to 200,000 per month.

**[02:31:24.820] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002660)  
So, I think nabibigyan po namin sila ng,

**[02:31:27.680] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002661)  
gusto namin po humigis sa inyo,

**[02:31:30.000] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002662)  
ng audience, Mr. Kirno,

**[02:31:32.300] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002663)  
pagka after this, after this ano, after this.

**[02:31:34.980] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002664)  
Yung pagiging jockey po, I mean, it's a, ika nga, lucrative,

**[02:31:39.080] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002665)  
dahil mataas po yung 300,000, di ba?

**[02:31:43.100] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002666)  
Hindi po sila nakatapos ng pag-aaral,

**[02:31:45.040] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002667)  
nakapag-train lang sila dahil sa Philippine Racing Commission Academy,

**[02:31:48.220] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002668)  
at ngayon po ay nabibigyan sila ng ganyang.

**[02:31:50.860] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002669)  
Pero meron naman po yata, ika nga, weight and height standard po.

**[02:31:55.400] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002670)  
Meron po.

**[02:31:55.820] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002671)  
Hindi naman po pwedeng maging jockey na, kasi taas ko 5'11,

**[02:32:00.000] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002672)  
kailangan mo siguro eh, 5'4 yata pinakamatangkad mo dyan.

**[02:32:03.000] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002673)  
5'2, 5'3, 5'4, tapos po yung weight kinakailangan,

**[02:32:06.420] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002674)  
pag mag-uumpisa kang hinete, 52 kilos pababa.

**[02:32:09.540] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002675)  
Ah, okay.

**[02:32:10.620] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002676)  
So, naintindihan po namin yung kanilang pinagdadaanan,

**[02:32:14.160] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002677)  
dahil ang kabayo po, 450 kilos to 500 kilos ang kabayo,

**[02:32:17.620] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002678)  
at kung 52 kilos na sila, sila yung rume-renda rin sa kabayo.

**[02:32:20.900] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002679)  
Hawak-hawak nila yung renda na nagkakontrol sa bibig ng kabayo

**[02:32:24.120] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002680)  
para patakbo yung kabayo.

**[02:32:25.620] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002681)  
Tumatakbo ng 70 kilometers per hour sa open air.

**[02:32:28.940] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002682)  
Mahirap po yun, mahirap.

**[02:32:30.000] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002683)  
Mahirap po yun, nakatapak lang sila sa bakal na maliit.

**[02:32:32.900] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002684)  
So, yun po yun, pero nabibiyayaan naman sila.

**[02:32:35.600] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002685)  
Yung mga nag-upload po ng mga hinete.

**[02:32:36.700] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002686)  
Sa mga pag nag-na-disgrasya po, nahulog, nagkasakit, nabalian,

**[02:32:43.500] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002687)  
who pays for the hospitalization?

**[02:32:45.380] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002688)  
Meron po kaming insurance, no? Insurance.

**[02:32:47.480] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002689)  
At the same time, may incentive po ang Jockeys Associations of the Philippines.

**[02:32:53.040] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002690)  
Through the prices given, the share of prices given by Philippine Racing Commission,

**[02:32:58.740] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002691)  
nakaka-accumulate siya ng mga 12 to 14 million per year na kita nila.

**[02:33:05.140] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002692)  
Kung di sila gumustuhan ng dalawang taon, meron siya mga 25 to 28 million.

**[02:33:09.920] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002693)  
Ito po, kunwari na nahulog itong Jockeys, sino pong magsushoulder na yun?

**[02:33:14.100] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002694)  
Yung may-ari ng kabayo na sinasakyan niyo or Philracom ipapa-hospital po siya?

**[02:33:19.220] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002695)  
Yung pong insurance, meron ko agad 300,000 to 350,000 sa insurance.

**[02:33:23.260] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002696)  
Tapos yung pong sinasabi kong budget na nakukuha nila,

**[02:33:26.520] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002697)  
doon po rin sila kumukuha ng additional na budget para dyan.

**[02:33:30.400] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002698)  
Tapos yung mga may-ari po ng kabayo,

**[02:33:33.280] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002699)  
mga may-ayaman naman na may-ari, karamihan eh.

**[02:33:35.780] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002700)  
So nagbibigay din po sila.

**[02:33:38.500] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002701)  
Have you already addressed, sir, yung doubts ng mga bettors na may game-fixing po?

**[02:33:46.300] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002702)  
How do you address that? Aside from mag-iimbestiga kayo,

**[02:33:50.840] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002703)  
because uso-uso ito, about 10, 15 years ago,

**[02:33:54.600] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002704)  
somebody taught me how to bet dito po sa horse racing,

**[02:33:59.880] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002705)  
mga kasamahan ko dati sa media.

**[02:34:04.180] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002706)  
Alam na alam niya ko eh, ito, denia ito, ito, nag-usap itong dalawang jockey na ito,

**[02:34:08.200] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002707)  
kaya ganito, na paano ito?

**[02:34:11.200] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002708)  
I mean, nabawasan na ba yan?

**[02:34:13.920] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002709)  
Malaki na po ang kabukusan, comparado sa mga naka-down generation.

**[02:34:18.520] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002710)  
Dahil nga sa pagigipit ng Philippine Racing Commission,

**[02:34:22.260] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002711)  
pagbibigay ng suspension doon sa mga hinete,

**[02:34:24.600] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002712)  
na medyo naliligaw ng landas,

**[02:34:27.220] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002713)  
at nangihinayin mo po yung mga hinete na mawalan sila.

**[02:34:29.660] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002714)  
Kasi pag nasuspindi sila ng 6 months,

**[02:34:31.960] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002715)  
alam mo, 6 months, they're averaging 100,000 a month.

**[02:34:35.560] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002716)  
So, definitely, 600,000 for 6 months is a big loss to them and to their family.

**[02:34:42.620] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002717)  
Kasi meron na po tayong, actually, yung law para sa illegal, ano, illegal, no?

**[02:34:50.680] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002718)  
Game-fixing.

**[02:34:51.420] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002719)  
Game-fixing po.

**[02:34:52.340] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002720)  
Yun po yung pinaliliwag namin sa mga hinete,

**[02:34:54.600] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002721)  
mga trainers,

**[02:34:55.620] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002722)  
na pagka nahulihan sila talaga at nakaroon ng valid reason,

**[02:34:59.300] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002723)  
we are not just suspending them.

**[02:35:01.000] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002724)  
We are willing to elevate the case to the proper authority, the NBI,

**[02:35:05.220] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002725)  
for them to be filed a civil case to them.

**[02:35:08.260] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002726)  
Kaya doon po sila natatakot.

**[02:35:09.800] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002727)  
Naproba na po ng Senado yung game-fixing law, I think, 2 years ago.

**[02:35:14.260] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002728)  
So, yun po yung pinanalaban namin sa kanila.

**[02:35:16.680] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002729)  
Aba kayo magluloko, dahil ito po i-apply namin.

**[02:35:20.240] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002730)  
Hindi lang namin kayo sususpindi ng 1 year,

**[02:35:21.920] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002731)  
but i-elevate namin yung kaso,

**[02:35:24.040] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002732)  
doon sa proper government authority.

**[02:35:27.360] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002733)  
Nakikita niyo ba kung pinipigilan nung Jackie yung kanyang kabayo?

**[02:35:31.800] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002734)  
I mean, instead of largahan niya, pipigilan niya kasi usong-uso dati ang gano'n.

**[02:35:38.000] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002735)  
Sasabihin ng mga manunood, pinipigilan o pinipigilan nung Jackie yung kabayo?

**[02:35:42.040] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002736)  
Ngayon po, meron na po tayong introduced 3 years ago,

**[02:35:45.080] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002737)  
a drone shot, nasa taas po yung camera,

**[02:35:47.500] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002738)  
makikita po yung galaw ng siko, nung kabayo, nung hinete.

**[02:35:50.900] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002739)  
So, makikita. Kitang-kita po yun.

**[02:35:52.440] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002740)  
Nagka-close up po yun.

**[02:35:54.040] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002741)  
So, yun po yung implement na isa sa mga ginawa namin, yung drone shot.

**[02:35:58.260] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002742)  
So, nahihira pa rin silang pumigit o magpasara o magpaliko na alanganin.

**[02:36:02.720] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002743)  
Magnakitaan po yun, pinapatawag sila, motoprope po yun.

**[02:36:06.040] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002744)  
Yung mga nasa media po, sad to say,

**[02:36:11.480] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002745)  
nag-meeting kami ng mga ibang organizations, ng mga betors,

**[02:36:15.380] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002746)  
ang sabi nila sa amin,

**[02:36:16.280] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002747)  
Chairman, karamihan dyan, natatalo na ng 20 pesos,

**[02:36:18.960] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002748)  
mas maingay pa sa amin na tumataya kami ng 1,000.

**[02:36:21.180] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002749)  
E, hindi po natin maiwasan kasi,

**[02:36:24.040] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002750)  
nasa democratic country po tayo.

**[02:36:26.320] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002751)  
So, pinapaliwanag nalang namin sa kanila.

**[02:36:28.140] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002752)  
So, nagiging transparent kami sa kanila.

**[02:36:29.960] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002753)  
Yung state world report, pinapost namin sa aming social media.

**[02:36:33.060] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002754)  
At yung aming motoprope decision, binibigay din po namin sa kanila.

**[02:36:37.980] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002755)  
Sir, may we know the actions taken by Phil Racom on COA's findings,

**[02:36:42.160] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002756)  
particularly your underutilization po ng breakage fund.

**[02:36:47.300] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002757)  
Nagagamit po ba talaga ito ng Phil Racom?

**[02:36:49.580] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002758)  
Ano po itong breakage fund?

**[02:36:51.360] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002759)  
Yung breakage fund po, actually,

**[02:36:54.040] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002760)  
nama po yung tanong ninyo, napakaganda po yung tanong ninyo.

**[02:36:56.600] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002761)  
Yung breakage fund po, dapat po yan sa STAD farm eh.

**[02:36:59.440] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002762)  
Sa STAD farm allocation.

**[02:37:00.880] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002763)  
Ngayon, ah, yung dati po ang Philippine Racing Club doon po sa Santa Ana,

**[02:37:05.680] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002764)  
na nalipat po sa NAE,

**[02:37:07.360] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002765)  
ah, may utang pa po sa gobyerno yun, ha?

**[02:37:10.560] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002766)  
Dapat yun. Hindi naman nila bibigyan sa Philippine Racing Commission po yun eh.

**[02:37:13.600] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002767)  
Bibigyan nila kung sino yung may hawak ng STAD farm.

**[02:37:16.760] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002768)  
So, yun po yun.

**[02:37:17.680] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002769)  
Nasa, pwede lang po yung STAD book, yung STAD farm nasa bayi po ngayon,

**[02:37:22.160] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002770)  
sa Department of Agriculture.

**[02:37:24.200] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002771)  
Kung kukuha po ng gobyerno yan, malaking bagay po yan,

**[02:37:27.080] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002772)  
at may bibigyan doon sa, sa bayi,

**[02:37:30.280] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002773)  
or sa sino man namin may hawak ng STAD farm ngayon.

**[02:37:33.560] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002774)  
Offensive Funding.

**[02:37:35.480] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002775)  
Pero yan, dyan sa may UP bandadi po ba?

**[02:37:37.680] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002776)  
Opo, nalipat po sa, nalipat po sa LIPA.

**[02:37:40.960] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002777)  
Hawak po ngayon ng, ano yan, ng, ah, bayi.

**[02:37:43.880] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002778)  
Kaya lang nung nakaraang meeting, ah, the other day po, ando po ko sa Congress,

**[02:37:47.680] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002779)  
hinihiling din po namin, kasama doon sa, sa aming, ah, hiling na ilipat sa Philippine Racing Commission,

**[02:37:54.040] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002780)  
yung, ah, STAD farm operation.

**[02:37:57.240] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002781)  
Dahil meron po tayong binili na kabayo, dalawa pong important na kabayo,

**[02:38:01.440] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002782)  
nagkakaralaga po yan ng, ah, 8 million.

**[02:38:04.640] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002783)  
I think that was 2015 o 16, ah, nung si Sen. Villar po, Sen. Villar po ay nag-initiate yan.

**[02:38:11.840] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002784)  
Ah, sad to say po, ah, nung binisita namin doon sa STAD farm,

**[02:38:16.040] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002785)  
na pa, na pangasiwa nung, ah, bayi, ay, ah, mukhang napabayaan po yung kabayo, eh.

**[02:38:22.200] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002786)  
Hindi po nakakapag-produce ng maximum.

**[02:38:24.240] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002787)  
Earnings para sa gobyerno.

**[02:38:26.440] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002788)  
Ah, another question po, ah, yung prices po, who determines na ito, is it mat, mathematics?

**[02:38:35.640] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002789)  
Is it computed by a machine?

**[02:38:37.840] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002790)  
Computed by the people?

**[02:38:40.040] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002791)  
Kung magkano yung betors, magkano po yung mananalo?

**[02:38:43.240] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002792)  
Ah, for example, sa, ah, ah, double-double, or sa ikang trifecta, pentafecta, ah,

**[02:38:53.240] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002793)  
winner-take-all, who determines that, na ito yung premyo?

**[02:38:57.440] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002794)  
Magamit po tayo ng, ano, magamit po tayo ng, ah, international totalisator, no?

**[02:39:01.640] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002795)  
International totalisator po, ginagamit ng Amerika, ginagamit ng Europe, at lahat ng, ah, bansa sa buong mundo, no?

**[02:39:08.840] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002796)  
Ah, United Tote po ang pangalan, United Tote. So, yan po ay, ah, ah, hindi po pwedeng dayain.

**[02:39:15.040] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002797)  
So, bawat taya, bawat taya ng mananaya, ay, ah, makikita automatic, ah, competition,

**[02:39:20.840] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002798)  
kung magkano yung ibibigay sa double, sa porca, sa winner-take-all, dun po.

**[02:39:24.440] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002799)  
Naka, ano po yan, nakaprogram po yan. So, wala po makikita dyan, kahit sinkundaya po, wala po makikita dyan.

**[02:39:30.640] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002800)  
Ah, internationally acceptable. Ah, ah, United Tote po, pwede nyo pong i-research, ah, United Tote, ah, totalisator, yun po yung taga-handler niyan.

**[02:39:40.840] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002801)  
Bakit po may mga games, minsan may, may race, kunwari race 3, parang tatlong kabayo, apat na kabayo, anong, kung ano doon?

**[02:39:49.040] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002802)  
At meron namang karera na, siyempre.

**[02:39:50.840] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002803)  
Ano naman tatakbo? O walo? Pero meron ba, apat lang ang tatakbo?

**[02:39:55.040] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002804)  
Ah, sa 2-year-old na kabayo po, in the 2-year-old, kasi kukunti lang ang population yan, ah, pinapayagan po namin yung apat na kabayo.

**[02:40:02.240] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002805)  
Pero yung 4-year-old and above, o 3-year-old and above, maximum, minimum po is 6, 6, yun po yun.

**[02:40:09.440] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002806)  
Kasi sa pagkalimbawa, ah, this season of September, lumalabas na yung mga 2-year-old this year.

**[02:40:15.040] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002807)  
So, September to December, tumatakba yung mga 2-year-old. Pinapayagan po namin tumakba yung apat na kabayo.

**[02:40:20.840] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002808)  
So, pag na-scout yung isa, nagiging tatlo, pero tolerable naman po yun. Base po doon sa aming mandato. Base po doon.

**[02:40:30.640] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002809)  
Yung ating mga magagaling na jockeys, how true na napapirate po sa Saudi, ng mga hari, ng mga prinsipe?

**[02:40:39.840] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002810)  
Yung mga magagaling natin, Ray, ang naiiwan, hindi na masyado kagalingan, or mga bagong pasok, mga rookie, pero yung mga magagaling, dahil napakalaki ng sweldo,

**[02:40:49.040] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002811)  
napakalaki.

**[02:40:50.840] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002812)  
Ano nga, OFW po sila, may mga bakasyon. Anong ginagawa ng Philracom para, I mean, yung mga magagaling, sasakay pa rin naman dito at hindi na pupupunta sa abroad?

**[02:41:02.840] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002813)  
Actually, yung mga hinete na Pilipino ngayon na nag-aabroad, hindi po sila hinete doon. Exercise rider lang po sila doon. Hindi po sila jockey.

**[02:41:12.840] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002814)  
Meron po yung jockey siguro mga in the last 5 or 10 years, siguro tatlo lang yung naging hinete doon.

**[02:41:18.640] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002815)  
Jockey Bakaykay, jockey Zarate, jockey Abobo, no? Kunti lang po. Pero karamihan po sa kanila, exercise rider.

**[02:41:26.640] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002816)  
Pero yun po, since for the last 5 years, tumaas ng tumaas ang, ang ano, ang premium rito because sa power assistance, no?

**[02:41:36.640] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002817)  
Napagbigitan bumalik dito yung mga dating hinete yung naging exercise rider doon, dahil nakita nila, mas malaki pa yung kikitain nila dito compared sa abroad.

**[02:41:45.640] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002818)  
Napakalaki ng tax doon sa abroad.

**[02:41:48.640] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002819)  
Compared dito. So, bumabalik po. Marami na po bumabalik dito ng mga magaling hinete po.

**[02:41:54.640] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002820)  
Saan? Siyempre, iba rin po yung kasama nila, yung pamilya nila dito.

**[02:41:57.640] PHILRACOM Chairman Aurelio P. de Leon** (asr-002821)  
Definitely po.

**[02:41:59.640] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002822)  
Alright. Well, sir, there being, I have no more other questions.
